Action! — Pay-per-view

Players and Pawns 2002, 31 December 2002

31 December 2002
Content noticeA period document, served unedited. It carries the humor, language, and content typical of its era and community — some of it crude, dated, or offensive, including material that wouldn't be written today. See Curation.
Players and Pawns 2002 MCI Center — Washington, District of Columbia ---- PLAYERS AND PAWNS. PLAYERS AND PAWNS. Circle Jerk. Starring: Reed Young & The Referees. (The scene goes from the ten second Action Wrestling Introduction to Reed Young’s office, as he sits in his desk, grinning from ear-to-ear.) Reed Young: Hello world! And let me be the first man to welcome you to the Pay-Per-View that will not only PUT us on the map like never before... we will take the fWo OFF of it! (Young laughs.) Young: fWo what!? fWo who!? COUNTDOWN MY ASS! Tonight, I thank you... because you are supporting me, Reed Young, and for that, I will grin evilly! (He does.) Young: Tonight... I want you to laugh, cry, smile and cheer... but please, do not be shocked our actions tonight. For tonight I welcome you to the last show of 2002... as you will be spending New Year’s Eve with us.So I just wanted to say that- **Knock knock knock** Young: ::Sighing:: Come in. (The camera turns... and there, every single Action Wrestling Referee stands.) Young: Yes? You just interrupted my promo... I’d honestly close the Fed down too because of this, but I need to show people what I can do to LLB and Christopher Fox tonight... so, maybe, I’ll close it down after. Anyway, what do you boys want? (The Owner takes a pencil out of his holder and begins biting away on the eraser part of it.) Referee One: Look, Reed, we know it’s only been a week since the event... but can you please hire James Jack back? The way you fired him two weeks ago... it really wasn’t right of you to do something like tha- Young: OB-WHATEVER-THE-FUCK-I-SAY-JECTION! (The Referee’s look at each other confused.) Young: You want him back? (They all nod.) Young: You miss him? (They all nod again, as one of them spoke.) Referee Two: With all due respect, sir, James Jack has been with some of us since PIW Era Two... that’s where you signed us after all. You didn’t want to have cheap Ref’s, you wanted the best for your rat- Young: Enough! Referee Two: Sorry. (The Owner tilts his head back to think about it for a moment or two... as the Referee’s watch each other with questions in their eyes... and high hopes in their hearts.) Young: You know... maybe, maybe I will. Referee Three: Thank you sir! Young: BBBUUUTTT... Referee One: But?... Young: If I was to do that for you... could you boys... do something... for me? (They all look at each other again, with more questions in their eyes. Their “ring leader” then replies.) Referee One: I guess we can. Young: Okay... because, if you do... I will give you James Jack back! You see it’s kind of like a circle jerk boys... you suck my dick, and I’ll eat your ass... Referee Two: We’re all straight, though. (Young snaps, shooting out of his chair.) Young: THAT’S NOT THE POINT! (He then sits back down in his leather chair.) Young: Besides, I’m straight too. I just meant to say it’s LIKE a circle jerk... okay? (They all nod once more.) Referee One: Okay. Referee Two: Yeah. Now tell us what you’d like us to do... and... ::looking around at all of the other Ref’s:: we can. (Young puts the easer part of the pencil back in his mouth, biting it off and spitting it out. He then turns to the camera once more... and grins evilly.) Young: Welcome to my Countdown... big time. (Pause.) Young: MUHAHAHAHAHAHAHAHAHAHAHAH- ---- Promo. Promo. Friends. It’s so nice to have so many of your friends around the holiday time. Spending Christmas with them... spending vacations with them... and now, spending New Year’s Eve with them. Especially the ones that just come home uninvited. Last Laugh, Action Wrestling, November 24th, 2002: Ump: !?!?!? DERIK- DERIK... THAT’S SEAN CYLEAR!! MY GOD... I THOUGHT HE WAS DEAD!! (Cylear tilts his head back... and laughs. The camera soars past his head... as it shows the roof of the hallway.) Ump: SEAN CYLEAR... WHAT THE HELL IS GOIN- They didn’t know he was alive. They didn’t think he would return... but at the Pay-Per-View... he sought what was his. He got his last laugh. Countdown to Insanity, Pinnacle of Insane Wrestling, December 31st, 2000: Brown: Cylear’s just run LLB into that car... as Johnson makes sure the door is locked!! Shaw: WHERE THE HELL ARE THEY GOING!?!? Both JJ and Cylear are running away from the scene!! Brown: IT’S ALMOST NEW YEAR’S, TOO, DERIK!! IT’S ALMOST NEW YEAR’S!! Shaw: Johnson’s gonna count it down! MY YEAR IS COMPLETE! Jim Johnson: THREE! Johnson: TWO! Johnson: ONE! **CCCRRRASH!** Brown: MY GOD... MY GOD A FUCKING WRECKING BALL JUST DROPPED ON TOP OF THE CAR!! LLB’S INSIDE THAT!! LLB MAY BE DEAD!!! Sean Cylear: Well, at least I got to see the ball drop... It was the moment that took LLB out of the wrestling world for two whole months. It was the moment that changed his life. For he... came close... To dieing. At the hands of his former friends. ‘Showcase’ Sean Cylear and Jim Johnson. Massacre, Pinnacle of Insane Wrestling, April 14th, 2001: Brown: Johnson has the chair in his hand... WAIT A SECOND!! JJ just nailed ‘SHOWCASE’ WITH THE CHAIR!!! What the hell... Johnson hits ‘Showcase’ with it again!!! AGAIN!! AGAIN!!! LLB slowly getting to his feet... holding his ribs! He has an evil smile on his face... something is fishy! Shaw: LLB has the chair now... ‘Showcase’s’ getting up... AND LLB NAILS HIM WITH THE CHAIR!! Brown: LLB COVERS!!! Johnson counts! Brown: One! Brown: Two! Brown: Three! Shaw: What just happened!? Brown: Johnson has handcuffs in his hand!!!! JJ’s cuffing ‘Showcase’ to the cage as LLB has the chair again!! JOHNSON’S ORDERING LLB TO KICK HIS ASS!!! **WHACK!** **WHACK!** **WHACK!** **WHACK!** Shaw: ‘Showcase’ is LIFELESS!!! And Look at LLB! Johnson has a mic... getting right into ‘Showcase’s’ face... Johnson: DID YOU THINK I MEANT YOU... you dumb son of a bitch when I said I was getting the old band back together? I MADE YOU... I MADE JOEY STARR!!! I MADE OVERDOSE... I ADVISE YOU ALL to show up next week... I guess you know who my lawyer is now huh? It was the turn that drove Sean Cylear mad. Inside a steel cage... with his friend Jim Johnson as the Guest Referee on PIW’s return. It was also the turn that put his career in the garbage. Pressure Point, Action Wrestling, December 16th, 2002: Ump: THE CAGE HAS LOWERED... **WHACK!** **WHACK!** **WHACK!** **WHACK!** Ump: CYLEAR’S BEING THE HELL OUT OF LLB INSIDE THAT HELL IN A CELL!! Shaw: And no matter what... despite ALL of the Referee’s trying to open the Hell in a Cell up... THEY WON’T BE GETTING IN!! HAHAHAHAHAHA!! Revenge. Even sweeter when no one can stop you. Pressure Point, Action Wrestling, December 2nd, 2002: Young: Welcome to your new hell, Roland! And just to show you what you’re in for, at ‘Players and Pawns’... your best friend, Christopher Fox, will die first! Will die... facing Sean Cylear!! Ump: THAT- THAT’S MASSIVE!! Shaw: Those two haven’t fought in YEARS! Even more-so than LLB and Cylear!! Ump: Look at the smile on Cylear’s face, too... that’s class, THAT’S FUCKING CLASS YOU SCUM BAG!! But before full revenge can be sought... a little bit of suffering would never truly hurt. Pressure Point, Action Wrestling, December 9th, 2002: Young: It’s all a work in progress, Roland. I simply have Cylear kill off old Fox here... and *then* the logical thing is for him to go after you. It’s like any good movie... its still got its climax. LLB: If I can’t have Cylear... I want you. Pressure Point, Action Wrestling, December 23rd, 2002: Ump: That’s LLB! He’s behind Reed Young... and Young turns, seeing LLB there... **CRASH!** Ump: ‘ERRONEOUS CONCLUSION’ THROUGH THE LEGO TOMBSTONE! Shaw: I DON’T BELIEVE IT!! LEGO... LEGO... EVERYWHERE!! Come Tuesday... it’s on. Family. The best and the worst... the ones that should always be there for you. If friends do not succeed... at least family can. Or should. Pressure Point, Action Wrestling, October 28th, 2002: (Keri Lindum raised the chair, as both men suddenly realized she was there, and stopped the fighting. She hesitated. Then...) **SMAAAAACK!!!** Ump: WHAT THE HELL!? Shaw: WHAT THE HELL!? Ump: Did she... she just hit Joey Malone! Her HUSBAND! Shaw: Swerve, baby! Swerve! So much for eternal love. Pressure Point, Action Wrestling, December 23rd, 2002: Kent Anthason: You ant a damn thing anymore, Fission. You can’t even get these Fans pissed of and mad! I can though. And I don’t even need to kidnap someone’s girlfriend to do it... she just attracts herself to me! Pressure Point, Action Wrestling, December 23rd, 2002 (later on): Joey Malone: Sorry? For what you did? This is just a bit late, Keri. Way too late. So, you know what? I’m done. I’m done moping around, hoping to have you back. I’m done watching you pretend that you love Kent more than you loved me. I’m done sitting back and watching my Wife groping a asshole like Kent. (Pause.) Malone: I’m over you. Players and Pawns, Action Wrestling, Tonight: And so former friends and family members square off against each other tonight... the thoughts of revenge seeping throughout their minds... For they would do anything to get what they want. And tonight... only one plan can work. Only one man can reach their checkmate... But which one, is that going to be? ---- Introduction. Introduction. (The promo changed to the MCI Center as fireworks went off in front of the entrance, as the Fans all stood, cheering like mad while their signs flew up in the air as they did. The Pay-Per-View theme, “With You” the Remix by Linkin Park was heard... as the camera changed again, to see the band playing live and to the right hand side of the walkway, on plane with the entrance, as the Crowd got even louder. The scene then went to the entrance again, as it showed the layout. A ramp-less walkdown, with a caged-like net standing a good fifteen feet tall holding the Action Wrestling logo behind it on a wall, positioned in the middle of the net. Beside the caged-like fence, to the right hand side was a giant gray-to-white painted king chess piece standing at about thirty feet in the air. It was big, thick, and made of wood, along with the pawn piece on the left hand side. Instead though, this piece was black, and only stood at about fifteen feet, so it’s on an even playing field with the caged-like net. Above all of this, finally lied the giant Action Screen. The scene then went to the ring, showing the layout there. The pathway to the ring was just black... but had yellow traffic lines across the flooring... only to be painted brown. The canvas was a normal mat, with black turnbuckle padding and light brown faded ropes... along with the aprons that read “Players and Pawns” in the Pay-Per-View logo. The camera changes one last time, as the fireworks and Linkin Park die down, to show Liam Ump and Derik Shaw sitting behind the announce table.) Liam Ump: Hello and welcome to ‘Players and Pawns’! I’m Liam Ump... Derik Shaw: I’m Derik Shaw... the cool, hip, SEXXXXY one! I’m blonde too. Ump: And tonight we’re ready to go on one HELL of a journey... even to celebrate New Year’s Eve at the same time! Shaw: Fun... oh so much fun... Ump: He’s sarcastic already! Shaw: Me? Naa. Ump: Anyway, Bobby Minio and Sweet Aroma will lock horns... as well as Invalid and Noah Hawkins in the right to own each others paychecks! Except, it’s a Hawkins’ paycheck times two! Shaw: We have the A, B, C and D Titles all on the line tonight! The Elements take on Arcade & Son... whereas Simon Seaman defends against both members of team V.I.A.G.R.A.! Ump: Add on a Triple Main Event too, plus MUCH much more... this is going to be a killer night! Shaw: So, ah, what the hell... let’s get started! Ump: We’ll be with you for the next FOUR hours folks... enjoy! Shaw: FOUR HOURS!! I THOUGHT IT WAS THREE!! Ump: We’re starting at eight, now. Shaw: Four hours... (Derik looks over to Liam, sticking his right index finger out.) Shaw: WITH YOU!? Ump: I know. I hate it too. Shaw: Well, that’s good then... Ump & Shaw: ... Shaw: At least we both agree. ---- Never Contemplated. Never Contemplated. Starring: Airborn Tyler. (A taxi pulled up in the dimly lit parking lot. The black cab looked out of place amongst the numerous sport cars and limousines, but it moved on as soon as the inhabitant threw open the door and leapt out. Smelling the fresh Washington air, he raised his head to the ceiling and sniffed. The crowd cheered. In an ironic way they kind of liked this man.) Airborn Tyler: WOOOOOOOOO! (Sure, his attitude sucked, but in the ring he was one of the most entertaining cruiserweights on the face of the Earth. As he grabbed his light back, he slammed the door of the taxi shut, and as the driver put his foot on the accelerator pedal, Airborn approached the rear of the arena and the revolving doors. He shuffled through them, talking to himself.) Airborn: Conrad Ramsey thinks he’s better than me? Pah, I’ll show him. The old dude won’t know what hit him. I could beat him in less time than he consumes to walk down the ramp. (In high spirits, he stumbled into the rear entrance, which was luxuriously equipped. The soft red floor and bright dull orange wall paper blended in well with the expensive light shades, and the polished mahogany desk at the front also lent itself to the executive atmosphere. Airborn felt out of place though and continued to walk through.) Airborn: I hope I have extra room in my bag for the Bantam Championship, lord knows… (But Airborn was cut off. As he’d been walking with his head down he hadn’t noticed the guy in a leather bright orange jacket in front of him. Airborn gasped, as the man winked and flashed his dazzling white smile.) Jon Bon Jovi: *singing* Shot through the heart, and you’re to blame, Airborn you give love, a bad name. Airborn: AHHHHH! IT’S JON BON JOVI! (Jon continued to wink and smile, showing off the brightest teeth in America. The man who’d sold 93,000,000 albums was in Washington.) Jovi: *singing* You’re an asshole, from the turnbuckle you’ll fly! And you’re wanted… (Richie Sambora, the leading guitarist and backing singer for the band Bon Jovi dove into the picture.) Richie Sambora: *shouting* WWWWAAAAAANNNNNNTTTTTEEEEEEDDDDDD! Jovi & Sambora: *singing* Dead or alive! Airborn: Tell me about it guys, tell me about it. Everyone in this company wants a piece of me just because I’m the cousin of the Dyad Champions. Jovi: *singing* Well we’re half way there! Sambora: *shouting* OOOOOOHHHHHHHHHH! Jovi & Sambora: *singing* LIVIN’ ON A PRAYER! (Airborn still looked on in amazement. This was surreal.) Airborn: My prayer is walking out of here still the A Star Student, and the NEW Bantam Champion. Jovi: *singing* But you could be shot doowwwnnn, in a blaze of glory! Airborn: No! (With a pat on the shoulder, Jon Bon Jovi and Richie Sambora disappeared up the stairs that lead to the private boxes, leaving Airborn to contemplate what he’d just heard.) Airborn: But, I can’t lose! I’m Airborn Tyler, he’s Conrad Ramsey! HE’S THREE TIMES AS OLD AS ME! (Realizing doubt had been placed in his mind, Airborn set off through the corridors in search of reassurance.) ---- Invalid vs. Noah Hawkins Invalid vs. Noah Hawkins “My Heart Will Go On” by Celine Dion plays over the PA system as highlights of Noah Hawkins and Invalid’s feud roll on the Action-Tron. Every night in my dreams I see you, I feel you, That is how I know you go on. Far across the distance and spaces between us You have come to show you go on. On screen we see Invalid and Hawkins walking toward each other from opposite directions. With no warning, Invalid lands a stiff right hand on an unsuspecting Hawkins. The footage rewinds and shows the attack from many different angles in slow motion. It then shows Hawkins laid out on the floor bleeding. Then a voice over begins to speak above the visuals. Voice Over: And with this vicious unprovoked attack, a rivalry begins. Invalid would not stand for Hawkins uncalled for assault on his fist with Noah’s face. And as if to taunt him, Hawkins lays there. Bleeding. MOCKING him. Neeeeeaaaar! faaaaaaaar! whereeeeeever you arrrrrreeeeee! I believe that the heart does gooooooon! The footage now shows Invalid in the ring calling out Hawkins, telling him and the fans in attendance about the “interesting” spin he took on the attack a show earlier. The confrontation between the two men, breaks out into an impromptu tag-team match. Which Hawkins and Invalid won. Voice Over: The kind hearted Invalid decides to forgive and forget and take Noah Hawkins under his wing as a team mate. But, Noah, for a lack of a better description...sucks. Clips of Noah getting his ass kicked my Max Danger and Bobby Minio, then by Jeff Garvin in the Virus Match at the PPV are shown on screen...with the parts where Invalid leaves the match or doesn’t care to provide support to his team-mate conveniently edited out. Once more, you open the door And you're here in my heart, And my heart will go on and on. Voice Over: Enough was enough. Invalid did his best, but he couldn’t carry this youth anymore. Noah, angered at Invalid for severing the ties between them, pleads for a match. Invalid disagrees. The video screen cuts to the footage of Invalid’s brutal attack on Hawkins in a ladies rest room. We see Hawkins face bloodied after being bashed into the toilet seat and given a few swirlies. The footage is capped off with Invalid place kicking him into “la la” land. Neeeeeaaaar! faaaaaaaar! whereeeeeever you arrrrrreeeeee! I believe that the heart does gooooooon! Voice Over: Invalid sent Noah a message. He clearly did not want to be bothered. But Hawkins would not take a hint. After begging for while, he finally came up with terms Invalid would agree to. Invalid’s paycheck on the line for 2 of Noah’s. You're here, there's nothing to fear, And I know that my heart will go on. We'll stay forever this way. You are safe in my heart, And my heart will go on and on. Voice Over: Finally, Noah Hawkins gets his wish. He will face Invalid in a No DQ match. Sadly for Noah, this match will not only cost him his dignity...but his means of living as well... The lights go back on, and the video screen shuts off. Ump: ... Ump: ... Ump: WHAT THE HELL WAS THAT!?!? Shaw: It’s BRILLIANT is what it is. Only Invalid would be able to capture the essence of this feud the way he did. I repeat... brilliant! Ump: Did it not seem a little one-sided to you Derik? Shaw: Yes. And it was the correct side dammit. Ump: Whatever. “Feel Good” by hed (p.e.) blasted through out the arena as Hawkins angrily stormed to the ring, the fans cheering him on, but him ignoring them completely. He slides into the ring and paces back and forth, staring down the entrance ramp, in wait of Invalid. Shaw: Damn. He seems pissed. Ump: I don’t think he took Invalid’s little pre-match production too well. “Comfortably Numb” by Pink Floyd seeps out of the arena amps, as Invalid strolls down the ramp toward the ring, receiving a healthy chorus of boos from the fans. He stops a few yards in front of the ring staring back at Noah Hawkins, a malevolent grin displayed on his face. Ump: That pre-match highlights really bothered me. Shaw: Why? Ump: There’s something about Celine Dion’s vocals being played over visuals of a grown man having his face rearranged by a toilet seat that I find...a bit unsettling. Shaw: Oh... well you’re just a sissy then. Invalid remains outside the ring, pointing and laughing of the “X” slashed across Hawkins’s face due to a little encounter with The Freak on the Asylums “Show”. Noah was livid and beckoned Invalid to enter the ring. Ump: Ohhhh. That’t a nasty looking scar. Shaw: See. If Noah had gotten what Invalid was trying to tell him before, he wouldn’t have gotten into beef with those TFZ fellas, and his face wouldn’t look like a box in a tic-tac-toe game. Invalid however stayed right wear he was. Noah decided to meet him outside, launching himself over the top with a suicide plancha. A badly telegraphed suicide plancha. Invalid caught him and dumped him back first into the guardrail with a fall away slam. Noah scrambled to his feet only to get kneed in the gut by Invalid. Invalid put Hawkins in a headlock and wailed away at him with solid shots to the face. He reared back for one last punch that connected squarely with Noah’s face, and sent him flying backwards. The ref figured they’d probably never make it into the ring so asked for the bell to be rung. **DING DING DING** With that Invalid lifted Hawkins to a vertical base. He went for a grapple but Noah pushed off and backed away. Noah charged in on Invalid and slammed into him, pinning him between the steel guardrails. Hawkins started to thrust his shoulders into Invalid’s midsection, but Invalid fought out of it with solid forearms to the back of Noah’s neck. Noah relinquished his grip. Invalid snapped Hawkins upright with a kick to the face, and clotheslined him down. Ump: Invalid is taking it to Hawkins early on in this match. I’m rooting for Hawkins to win, but with the way Invalid is handling him here, it doesn’t look to good for him. Shaw: Gee... ya think? Invalid grabbed Hawkins while still on the ground and wheelbarrow suplexed him over his head. Invalid then left Hawkins, and began digging under the ring. Ump: What’s he looking for? Invalid pulls out and tosses aside a chair, a table, a trashcan lid, a trashcan, a led pipe, some barbed wire, then finally he got hold of what he was looking for. A cheese grater. Shaw: What’s he gonna do with that? Ump: I dunno. But knowing Invalid, it won’t be pretty. Invalid grabs the cheese grater and leers toward Noah Hawkins just getting up to his feet. Invalid takes him back down with a kick to the ribs, and mounts him. Invalid holds the cheese grater up to Noah Hawkins, as Noah’s face goes white with horror. Ump: Jesus Christ! I think I know what about to happen! In a sickening display, Invalid mashes the cheese grater on Noah’s face vigorously swiping up and down, peeling away at the fresh scabs he received courtesy of The Freak and his pal Mr. Switchblade, and added even more lacerations. Noah’s face oozed blood, and Invalid continued with ruthless assault with glee gleaming in his eyes. Ump: Oh man...I think I’m gonna be sick. Shaw: That’s fucking sadistic. I don’t think I’ve ever seen anyone do that before. Invalid lets up, and takes a moment to admire his gruesome masterpiece. Hawkins’s face was a mask of crimson and tattered flesh. Invalid lifted him up and whipped him into the railing. Invalid rushes in and lands a spinning wheel kick that sends both him and Hawkins toppling over into the fans. Security rushes in to keep the fans from interfering. Hawkins is struggling to get up. Invalid lends him a helping hand, then a helping fist to the jaw. Hawkins’s reeled back, and Invalid charged in with a shoulder block sending him into a mess of seats and fans. Invalid lifts Hawkins out of the mess, and throws him down to the concrete. Hawkins thrives in pain, as Invalid turns his attention elsewhere. Ump: Oh man. This is brutal. Shaw: Well it serves Hawkins right. He should’ve never wasted Invalid’s precious time. Invalid grabs a few cups from fans nearby, and examines them. He tosses the cups aside spilling beer on the floor. Obviously the fans don’t like this and are trying to get at him, but security is doing a wonderful job of holding them back. Invalid finally finds the beverage he was looking for: a medium sized cup of a dark brown liquid. Possibly whiskey or gin. Stolen cup in hand, he walks over to Hawkins, and nudges him in the ribs. Hawkins flips on his back to look up at Invalid, when... **Splash!** Hawkins: AAAAAAAAAHHHHHHHHH!!!! Invalid douses Hawkins’s face, covered in open sores with the liquor. In case you’re unsure of why Hawkins is screaming so loud, here is an equation to clarify the situation. Strong alcohol + Large open wounds = A fuckload of pain. Hawkins grabs at his face desperately trying to cope with the immense burning, while Invalid looks on grinning. Ump: MY GOD! I thought I’ve seen sadistic before, but this isn’t human. Noah doesn’t deserve this. No one does. Shaw: DUDE!! They serve hard liquor at these shows!?!? Ump: ... Your compassion for your fellow man never ceases to amaze me Derik. Invalid grabbed Noah Hawkins, dumped him over the railing again, then hopped over himself. Noah’s face was dripping all over the protective padding ringside, and Hawkins himself was still grimacing in pain. Invalid clutched him by the back of the neck, and dragged him toward the ring, shoving him under the ropes like shitty luggage. Invalid also stepped into the ring. But not before bringing a steel chair with him. Ump: Dear god! Someone stop this already. Shaw: You think they serve tequila? I could really go for some right now. Ump: ::Sighing:: Dammit Derik. Invalid came down hard across Noah Hawkins’s back with the chair, flattening Noah to the mat, leaving a bloody face print on the canvas. Invalid raised the chair again, and seemed to be coming down again onto Hawkins’s bloodied body, but halted mid-swing. Chair still in his hand, he went over to the turnbuckle and slumped in the corner. Digging into his pocket, he pulled out a blunt, lit it and did nothing. Ump: What the hell is he doing now? Shaw: Now I don’t know much about Invalid...but I do know enough to tell you how futile it is to ask questions like that of him. Minutes pasted. Noah was still struggling to regain composure, Invalid was still smoking a rabidly shrinking blunt, and the crowd was becoming extremely irritated at the idleness in the ring. Ump: Man, why doesn’t Invalid just end this thing? Shaw: Bah. He will... eventually. Using the ropes, Noah pulled himself up to his feet and lurched toward Invalid. Invalid put out the rest of his blunt, tucking the roach in his pocket for later, got up holding the steel chair menacingly, and dared Hawkins to come closer. Hawkins lurched forward, dipping red along the way. Hawkins went face to...well...chest with Invalid who had 6 inches on him. Ump: You gotta respect Noah Hawkins’s heart right here. He’s beaten to a pulp, clearly being out fought right here, but he’s STILL standing up and getting right into this bastards face. Amazing! Shaw: ... He’s still gonna get his ass kicked though. Ump: Well yeah, but he’s taking it remarkably well. Invalid rears back and swings the chair...but Noah ducks. Noah now attacks Invalid like a wounded animal, which really isn’t too far form the truth right now, pelting Invalid with fist after fist. Invalid drops the chair, and is helpless against the onslaught. Ump: YES!! YES!! NOAH’S FIGHTING BACK!! THE MOMENTUM IS TURNING!! Then Invalid low blows him, and pump-handle slams him down. Shaw: So much for that. Invalid picks the chair back up and- **SMACK!** **SMACK!** **SMACK!** **SMACK!** Begins raining down relentlessly on Noah Hawkins. He lifts the chair high over his head and comes down on more brutal time to Hawkins’s skull. **SMMMMMAAAACK!!** Invalid tosses the dented and bloody chair aside, leaving Noah to tend to the re-opening, of his re-opened wounds. Invalid lets his body tumble over the ropes, reaches under the ring and slides 2 tables into the ring. Ump: This is a horrible display. Invalid’s gone to far. Noah Hawkins never even did anything THAT bad to him. Matter of fact, he’s done nothing at all. Invalid’s been the agitator from day one. Shaw: That’s BS and you know it! Invalid goes to set up the tables, but as he sits it up on 2 legs, he decides against it, and kicks it back down to a flat base. He rips Hawkins’s to his feet, and body slams him down on the table. Ump: What the hell!? Shaw: Don’t you see!? If Hawkins goes through the table he can only use it once, and it crumbles. If he lays it flat, he can slam Hawkins into hardwood till the fucking cows come home. I love this guy! Invalid lifts Noah Hawkins onto his shoulders and drills him into the flat table with a Death Valley Driver. Invalid then kneels down and slams Hawkins’s head into the table repeatedly. Invalid then pulls Noah to his feet, and applies a standing head scissors. He lifts him up and slams Noah down with a sloppy looking powerbomb, landing Noah awkwardly on the wooden table. Invalid kicks Noah off the table and onto to canvas. Invalid sets up a table, and grabs the other one. He sets it up, and places it on top of the previous one, double stacking them. He then nudges Noah’s limp body toward a corner. He hoists him onto the top, climbs to the second rope, and double underhooks Noah Hawkins. Ump: Dear God! Noah however shows signs of life, and begins struggling feverishly. Invalid climbs all the way to the top looking to gain leverage, and before you know it both men are balancing delicately on the top rope. Hawkins’s however has slightly better footing, and uses it to his advantage. The two men grapple with each other, until... **CRASH!** **CRASH!** Both men went flying off the top rope and through both tables. Unfortunately for Invalid, he served as the human pillow cushioning Noah’s fall, as their combined body weights drove him through both tables. Ump: YES!! YES!! THE MOMENTUM!! IT’S SHIFTING!! Shaw: You do remember what happened the last time you said that, don’t you? Noah lay, exhausted, on top of Invalid. It took a while for the ref to realize this was technically a pin. Malone decided to run to the ropes, and catapult himself towards Fission with designs of hitting him with a flying forearm. Syphon, however, had other plans then absorbing that blow. Fission grabbed him, and gave him a wicked Butterfly suplex. Shaw: Hrmph~! This is so boring without Kent. Ump: Fission with that butterfly suplex, nullifying Malone's attempt at using that forearm. Malone shot right back up, pissed that his moves were failing him with a man who was well known to be technically inferior to him. Fission was always about pummeling you till you couldn't kick out. Add a new wrinkle to him. Malone did the first thing that came to his head. He kicked Fission right in the gut, and while the big man was dazed, he did what came flowing into his brain. Ump: Japanese Ocean Cyclone suplex on Fission! Fission however, stood right back up. Shaw: Look at Malone. He's shocked...serves him right for sucking. Malone was now flustered. That should have put him down, but it seemed to do little more then phase him. Fission just smiled through the crusted blood from his gash, and returned the kick to the gut favor. He then hooked him up for a suplex. Ump: Vicious snap suplex here. Fission, however, didn't let go. He pulled Malone back up with him, and executed another vicious snap suplex. Shaw: What the hell is this? Fission has no skill. How in the hell is he executing move after move? Fission didn't let go, and again snap suplexed Malone into the mat. And again. And again. Fission had just executed five consecutive snap suplexes on Joseph Tyler Malone, and it was taking its toll on both of them. Ump: Fission has Malone up again...A SIXTH STRAIGHT SNAP SUPLEX BY FISSION ON JOEY MALONE! Shaw: Pfft. You think that takes any talent. Malone is light years ahead of him... AND JOEY SUCKS! Fission let go of Joey, and laid on the mat, trying to get his strength back. Malone, however, was in a very different world. He had faced Fission countless times, but never did he seem to have this kind of surgical precision. There was always something he could do that could get under Fission's armor. Ump: Fission has come in and began dominating in basically every facet of the game. Fission got up, soaking up the cheers he was receiving for his little clinic. His wear down game plan was working at a more accelerated pace then he thought, but he could slow things down even more now. Shaw: Fission's climbed a single rope on that corner. What can you do from there? God...he is so pathetic. Fission measured Malone, and jumped up into the air, ready to drive his elbow and body into his intended target. Malone, however, had recovered from the snap suplex combo, and was now standing up. Fission knew he was in trouble when Joey grabbed him in mid air. Ump: EXPLODER SUPLEX ON FISSION! Malone has some life left in him! And Fission was on the mat, trying to register why he was in such pain. Malone needed to make a serious dent in the Man-O-War, though. Even if it cost him some brain cells and the desire to take Tylenol. Shaw: Will these two retards stop? Ump: Derik, the second you can gain a following in the squared circle, then you can talk. Until then, WILL YOU PLEASE SHUT UP?! Derik Shaw could only grunt as Joey climbed the closest turnbuckle and measured the wounded duck in the ring. Ump: You've got to be kidding me here. Shaw: You can stop playing stupid at anytime, Liam. Malone's going to screw himself up, but he might as well do the same to Syphon Fission. Maybe the guy will go off and kill himself... Ump: STOP RAMBLING! Malone jumped, and man, did he get air. Malone flew with the greatest of ease, and landed a beautiful swan dive head butt. Ump: BAD MOON RISING! Malone would have smiled. Actually, he did for a good five seconds, until the pain crept back into his cranium. He rolled over, draping his arm over Syphon in hopes that the move had done more damage to the opponent then it did to him. James Jack slid down, and made the count. One! Two! THRE- Why dashes? Because Anthason had just broke the pin. Pissed as ever, he called for Lucas to intercede in the match, to make things look real good for him. Lucas made his way into the ring, and the fans went back into booing mode. Malone and Fission began to stir, but it didn't matter. Anthason picked up Joey, and started hammering him with rights and lefts and Devol picked up Fission and threw him into the corner. Shaw: DEVOL AND ANTHASON OWN! THE WINNING TEAM! YES...OH GOD YES! Devol began firing fist after fist at Syphon, while Joey felt each and every punch and kick from the Structure and the Method. Ump: Come on, this is too damned cheap. So? Anthason was now in control of the match, and threw Malone out of the ring. He then ordered Devol go and take care of Joey, which he went to do. That left Fission and Anthason, two men who never really did see eye to eye, to iron out their differences. Anthason: This is for being so damn horrible. Anthason then hooked Fission up, and lifted him in the air, and released his ass. Shaw: HA~! FISSION TASTED HIS OWN MOVE, THE MERCURY EXPLODA! This is a great day to be alive! Anthason, however, lacked the strength Fission did, so he was amazed on how much energy he had just wasted. Devol as taking to kicking Malone while he was down, which wasn't really well received. Devol put no effort into this, as he would soon pay. Malone got a boot into the crotch of Lucas Devol, turning the man from a gloating jerk ass to a guy who was wondering if getting an erection was still a possibility in the future. Shaw: HEY! Liam, you've been bitching about travesties all night. What Joey just did, THAT is a travesty! Malone soon got up, and entered the ring. Fission was getting up, but Anthason was up and ready to go. Fission looked at the two men through the corners of his eyes, as Malone jumped high in the air and landed a standing drop kick to Kent Anthason, sending him crashing to the mat. Ump: Malone means business here. Nicely executed standing drop kick to Kent Anthason. Malone wasted no time in laying the boots to Kent Anthason. However, the shadow of a large man was growing as he stomped away. Ump: Fission is going to do some big time damage now. Fission looked at Malone, then Anthason for a second. Shaw: WHAT ARE YOU STARING AT YOU BIG STUPID OX? KILL MALONE! Fission eyed Joey, and then began stomping away at Anthason with him. Shaw: DUMB FREAKING OX! YOU SHOULD GET TO BEATING UP MALONE AND KILLING YOURSELF RIGHT NOW! This continued for a couple seconds, until Fission decided it was time to totally mess up Malone. Fission clotheslined Malone as hard as he could, sending him to the mat with a loud thud, the fans roaring their love for the Man-O-War. Fission then picked Malone up in a fireman's carry, and twisted the move into a Michinoku Driver. Fission landed the move, and Malone was left to ponder how nice the black vale of unconsciousness would be. Ump: FISSION HIT THE MALONESTROM OF JOEY MALONE! THIS HAS TO BE IT! Fission went on to cover Malone, as James Jack slid and began the count. One! Two! THR-Kickout Malone. The fan settled down, now angry with what had just happened. Fission could only hit the mat with his fist as Anthason got up. He picked up the nearby chair, and lunged towards Fission. Shaw: YES! GO ANTHASON! **BAM~! Ump: FISSION JUST HIT ANTHASON WITH THE SURE SHOT~! The chair is out of the ring! Anthason is down! FISSION IS PINNING AND LISTEN TO THESE FANS!!! They were going apeshit, as Fission rolled over and made the pin, James Jack now making the count. One! Two! Leg drop by Devol! Leg drop by Devol? Lucas had once again messed up the chances of Fission to end this match. And then he exited, as if nothing had happened. Shaw: Fission is choking as we speak! Yeah! Ump: Obviously we aren't calling the same match. Both Malone and Fission have taken Kent Anthason to school here. Shaw: NO THEY HAVEN'T! Malone was now up, looking from side to side, seeing Fission up against the ropes, resting, and Anthason now getting on his feet. Creep. Radiohead. It began to play, and everyone stopped doing stuff and things and looked at the entrance. Fission, Anthason, and Malone stared at the entranceway. Devol stood on the outside with that steel chair in hand. Then, suddenly, a flash of white hair and extremely baggy blue jeans jumped through the crowd on to the guardrail, and missile dropkicked Devol's chair into his face. He was done. Ump: THAT MAN JUST TOOK LUCAS DEVOL OUT OF THE EQUATION! Shaw: NO! NO! THIS ISN'T FAIR! DEVOL WAS JUST THERE TO MAKE SURE MALONE AND FISSION DIDN'T CHEAT! WHOEVER THAT IS, THEY DESERVE A KICK SQUARE IN THE ASS~! The music cut as the face of that shirtless man became evident. And with that, a massive pop. Neko Case. Neko raised his hands up in a fast manner to get the crowd going. This was his dream. And now, he was going to pay Joey Malone back for the total ass kicking he received earlier in the month. He picked up the chair off the ground and entered the ring. The crowd was in a frenzy. Neko would take out the champion, the Structure and the Method, and cement his status as one of the ultimate good guys. Fission would walk out champion. Ump: Neko wants a piece of Malone! He wants a piece of Anthason! Fission promised he would win for his protégé tonight! And goddammit, he's going to come through on his world. Shaw: I'm going to vomit. Fission rested on the ring ropes, trying to collect himself for one last onslaught as Malone and Anthason exchanged rights and lefts. When the two tumbled out of the ring, Neko waved from the ring for the two to get back and receive what they had coming to them. Ump: LISTEN TO THE FANS! THEY ARE BEHIND NEKO CASE! Fission began walking and was now side by side with the Flamboyant Youth. The fans roared. Shaw: This isn't right! STOP THIS NOW! Malone gained the upper hand, decking Anthason with a hard left hand, causing him to go to the ground. Unbeknownst of him, Neko waited by the ropes. And the second he climbed up to the ring apron... **BANG~!!!!!!** A RESOUNDING CHEER~!! GAME OVER~!!!! ... ... ... Ump: You've got to be kidding me...I'm going to be sick. Nope. An outpouring of boos as Fission's head and body snapped back violently onto the mat. Malone looked through his blood-crusted hair and was just in awe. He had never seen such a hard shot via chair in his life. Neko just stood over Fission, a wicked glow now emanating to him. Neko then threw the chair down and simple walked to the back, a huge grin on his face, as Joey Malone looked at the now lifeless form that was Syphon's body. Shaw: HELL YEAH! CASE IS AN ANTAGONIST~! Malone went inside the ring, and dived on top of Fission for the pin. Jack immediately went for the count. One! TWO! THRE- Anthason pulled James Jack to the outside, where he hit his head. Anthason entered, kicking Joey directly in the back of the head and causing him to roll over. Shaw: NOW ANTHASON! END THIS NOW! Ump: GOD DAMMIT~! Anthason mounted the top turnbuckle, and flipped off the dazed Joey Malone. And he jumped as the fans booed, his body spinning and contorting in the air. And then, he landed right on top of Joey. Double Helix. Shaw: YES~! YES~! OH GOD YES~! Anthason had some time to kill, and thought Joey was done. However, the monster that had just absorbed that chair shot from Hell was now trying to stand up on two feet. He struggled, but he was going to make things difficult for him. So he exited the ring. Ump: What's he doing here? The match is over. Anthason has this match over and he is letting time slip. A stupid move here. Shaw: THERE IS A REASON! TRUST ME! And much like at Last Laugh, Anthason pulled a sledgehammer from under the ring. He reentered and grabbed the chair that had been used the entire match. Ump: Fission's standing, but barely seems alive. Shaw: KILL HIM~! Anthason threw the chair at Fission. Syphon, without a thought, grabbed it and sat there like a moron for a few seconds. A few too many. Anthason swung. **SMASH CRUNCH THUD CRACK DEATH~!** Fission had just been made a bitch thanks to a successful Ender's Game. Jack came into the ring just in time to do nothing but watch. Shaw: GOLD!!!!!! Shaw: ... Shaw: GOLD!!!!!!!! With that, and Malone being out cold thanks to the Double Helix mere minutes ago, the match was sealed. Anthason pinned Fission, and Jack made the count. One! Two! THREE! Wait! Stop booing fans. Fission's foot rested firmly on the bottom ring rope, and Jack let Anthason know. Needless to say, Anthason was angry beyond belief. That move could kill a person, after all. But, it did little more then silence Fission for the time being. Shaw: NO WAY! ANTHASON IS THE CHAMPION! SOUND THE FAT LADY! THERE WILL BE A PARADE FOR THE DEVOTED ON BOUBORN STREET! Ump: Fission's foot was on the rope Derik! This match is still going on. MALONE'S UP! And while Anthason threw a Christian-like fit, Malone was up and ready to end this entire thing. He grabbed Fission and threw him out of the ring, and looked on at Anthason. Before Kent knew what was really going on, he was up in a fireman's carry. And as Malone started going for the Michinoku Driver, Anthason had one thought traveling in his mind. “Fuck!” Malonestrom. GAME...OVER! Malone pulled the leg, and Jack made a woozy count, the fans giving the mother of all mixed reactions. One! Two! THRE- lights out? Guerilla radio? Nah, just teasing. Shaw: What in the hell?! Hey! Turn the lights back on! It's creepy! The sound of thunder emanated throughout the arena, and lightning appeared on the Action Screen. The fans were confused, as the lighting buzzed around the screen. Numbers appeared. 00:00:00:10 Ump: Umm... I thought Keith Scott Zimmerman already debuted! 00:00:00:09 00:00:00:08 00:00:00:07 Shaw: Maybe he wants to crash the main event! That'd be pretty sweet. 00:00:00:06 00:00:00:05 00:00:00:04 00:00:00:03 00:00:00:02 00:00:00:01 00:00:00:00 Pyro. Explosions. The whole front of the entryway nearly exploded as the lights came up. The fans weren't sure how to react, but then, a message zapped its way onto the Action Screen. It was a simple message, consisting of only four words. “HAPPY NEW YEAR, MOTHER FUCKERS.” Ump: Uh, that was one hell of a new year's mes- And then... 'Downfall'. No, not the fWo wrestler. The song by TRUSTcompany. Shaw: What in the HELL!? The entire arena buzzed. Who the hell used that song as a theme in Action!? Some cheered, some were confused. But all reacted. For while Joey Malone had a cover on Kent Anthason in the corner, an individual vaulted up to the top rope near them. He then leapt up and attained an incredible height as rushed toward Malone with a shooting star pre- No, wait, scratch that, shooting star LEG DROP. Naturally, the crowd went ballistic, and even more ballistic when it connected on the back of the head of Joey Malone. Ump: HOLY JESUS CHRIST! What in the HELL was that?! Who in the HELL is that!? The individual rose to his feet. And smirked his trademark smirk. Oh, come on, who the fuck do you think it was? Shaw: Li-Liam! That's... that's... THAT'S DANIEL PHILLIPS! From the Asylum! Phillips soaked in the cheers, and then... his impulses got the better of him. He dropped down on his hands and knees and yelled at the near-unconscious Joey Malone. Phillips: Come on, mother fucker! Not so Hall of fucking Fame-like now, are you?! Yeah, I FUCKING THOUGHT SO! You're flat on your fucking ass, and who's the reason for that? SURVEY SAYS... DANIEL... MOTHER FUCKING... PHILLIPS. You remember me, don't you, Joey? You FUCKING remember who I am. You might be unconscious and can't hear me, but I'm your god damned shadow. Wherever you go, I follow. I suggest you strap in, Joseph... because you're gonna see a lot more of me! A spit to the face and a boot to the head, and Daniel Phillips was back up. He was still talking trash, though you couldn't hear him now. He turned around to see Kent Anthason back up on his feet. Anthason seemed to be talking trash right back at Daniel Phillips, or maybe he was trying to convince Phillips to start a beatdown on Malone. Phillips seemed to really think about the latter. I believe the part where the firm denial came in was around this point. KICK. WHAM. PERFECT HALO. The crowd, for the lack of a better term, exploded, as Anthason's face and skull bounced sickeningly off of the mat following Phillips' wicked Implant Cutter. Ump: What the hell is Daniel Phillips doing here?! He's not remotely involved in this title match, which is STILL going on! Shaw: Do you think Daniel Phillips gives a flying crap? Phillips left the ring, still trashing talking his way back toward the curtain, but he still turned around just before he left to get a good look at what was about to happen. Oh, and he smirked again. I'm sure you knew that one before it was even mentioned. Syphon Fission re-entered the ring, none the wiser of Phillips' entry in the match. Ump: Syphon Fission! SYPHON FISSION! He was ejected from the ring earlier on! Fission saw the first body in the ring, and decided to strike. He pulled Kent Anthason to his feet, even though he was favoring his head. Then he executed a move that hadn't been seen in more than a half a year. Death Plunge. Ump: DEATH PLUNGE!!! DEATH PLUNGE IN THE CENTER OF THE RING! ANTHASON IS DOWN! Fission rolled over Anthason and made a lateral press. Moments later, the referee came to. Ump: ONE!!! Ump: TWO!!! Ump: THREE!!!!! **DING DING DING** Ump: NEW CHAMPION!!! Shaw: AAAHHH!! THE NIGHT IS COMPLETE! The fans' eruption nearly took the roof off the MCI Center and crashing down onto the White House in what probably would have been a deemed as a terrorist attack. The referee woozily got up to his feet. He then moved to the outside of the ring and grabbed the Action! Title from the timekeeper, and then handed the belt to Fission. Fission took the title and stared at it long and hard, looking at his own blood-soaked face in the reflection on the belt. He dropped to his knees and held the title. His journey since Pressure Point 1, since March 13th, 2002, when Action! Wrestling first hit the airwaved... when he had a less than ideal way to debut in the form of a double count out with Matt Remicho... was over. So was the three month Action! championship reign of Joseph Tyler Malone. So was any legitimate claim Kent Anthason could make for another shot at the Action! title. So was the year 2002. And in its place? The year 2003. A new champion to lead Action! Wrestling into its new year. And a new journey for Syphon Fission. A journey to defend his Action! title. A journey to atone for the sins and atrocities that he had committed in the first half of his Action! stay. A journey to be a true champion, once again. As Syphon Fission climbed up to the second turnbuckle and held the Action! title in the air, the fans roared. The champion, with sweat and blood pouring from the body, roared in his own right, holding his newly-won championship belt high into the air. Flashbulbs went off, covering the arena in bright flashes. Smoke from the pyro settled down around the floor of the arena, and all the while, the shock of the arrival of several new stars to Action! still lingered in the heads of the fans. What would the fallout be at the start of the new year? Outcome: Fission via Pinfall (over Anthason); NEW A Champion.
